```
	private static bool CheckMech(int i, int j, int time)
	{
		for (int k = 0; k < _numMechs; k++)
		{
			if (_mechX[k] == i && _mechY[k] == j)
			{
				return false;
			}
		}
		if (_numMechs < 999)
		{
			_mechX[_numMechs] = i;
			_mechY[_numMechs] = j;
			_mechTime[_numMechs] = time;
			_numMechs++;
			return true;
		}
		return false;
	}

	private static void XferWater()
	{
		for (int i = 0; i < _numInPump; i++)
		{
			int num = _inPumpX[i];
			int num2 = _inPumpY[i];
			int liquid = Main.tile[num, num2].liquid;
			if (liquid <= 0)
			{
				continue;
			}
			byte b = Main.tile[num, num2].liquidType();
			for (int j = 0; j < _numOutPump; j++)
			{
				int num3 = _outPumpX[j];
				int num4 = _outPumpY[j];
				int liquid2 = Main.tile[num3, num4].liquid;
				if (liquid2 >= 255)
				{
					continue;
				}
				byte b2 = Main.tile[num3, num4].liquidType();
				if (liquid2 == 0)
				{
					b2 = b;
				}
				if (b2 == b)
				{
					int num5 = liquid;
					if (num5 + liquid2 > 255)
					{
						num5 = 255 - liquid2;
					}
					Main.tile[num3, num4].liquid += (byte)num5;
					Main.tile[num, num2].liquid -= (byte)num5;
					liquid = Main.tile[num, num2].liquid;
					Main.tile[num3, num4].liquidType(b);
					WorldGen.SquareTileFrame(num3, num4);
					if (Main.tile[num, num2].liquid == 0)
					{
						Main.tile[num, num2].liquidType(0);
						WorldGen.SquareTileFrame(num, num2);
						break;
					}
				}
			}
			WorldGen.SquareTileFrame(num, num2);
		}
	}
```

```
// 行为无对应引擎系统的器件(音乐盒/派对/天塔柱等)按"帧翻转 + toast 占位"处理,注释标明。
import type { Wiring } from '../Wiring';
import { TILE_BY_KEY } from '../../data/tiles';

/** 器件运行环境(Game 实现;保持窄接口以便单测注入) */
export interface DeviceCtx {
  /** 按原版 item id 掉落物品 */
  dropItem(vanillaItemId: number, px: number, py: number): void;
  /** 按原版 npc id 生成敌人(雕像刷怪;返回是否成功) */
  spawnNpc(vanillaNpcId: number, px: number, py: number): boolean;
  /** NPC.MechSpawn（NPC.cs:7399-7483）：同 type（含等价组）200px≥3 / 600px≥6 / 全图≥10 拒绝 */
  mechSpawnOk(px: number, py: number, type: number): boolean;
  /** Item.MechSpawn（Item.cs:48982-49013）：同 item 300px≥3 / 800px≥6 / 全图≥10 拒绝 */
  itemSpawnOk(px: number, py: number, type: number): boolean;
  /** 投射物(飞镖/火焰/尖刺球/炮弹近似):vx/vy px/tick */
  shootProjectile(px: number, py: number, vx: number, vy: number, damage: number, kind: 'dart' | 'superdart' | 'flame' | 'spear' | 'spiky' | 'boulder' | 'geyser'): void;
  /** 统计附近某类投射物数量(尖刺球惩罚表用) */
  countProjectiles(kind: 'spiky', px: number, py: number, radius: number): number;
  /** 门开关(原版 HitWireSingle 10/11:随机方向+反向重试) */
  doorOpen(x: number, y: number): boolean;
  doorClose(x: number, y: number): boolean;
  /** 传送器对:搬迁判定框内的玩家/敌人(原版 Teleport :2314-2365) */
  teleportPair(a: [number, number], b: [number, number]): void;
  /** 城镇 NPC 搬迁雕像(style 40/41):group 0/1 白名单,搬到 (px,py) */
  relocateTownNpc(group: 0 | 1, px: number, py: number): boolean;
  /** 爆炸(炸药/地雷/炮弹):范围伤害+清格+粒子 */
  explode(tx: number, ty: number, damage: number, radius?: number, destroyTiles?: boolean): void;
  toast(msg: string): void;
  sfx(name: string, x?: number, y?: number): void; // 带坐标时按距离衰减/超距静音
  /** worldSurface(部分器件判定) */
  worldSurface: number;
}

/** 宝石块 off/on id 对(255-261 off ↔ 262-268 on,±7;Wiring.cs:863-873) */
const GEMSPARK_OFF = ['v_255_amethystgemsparkoff', 'v_256_topazgemsparkoff', 'v_257_sapphiregemsparkoff',
  'v_258_emeraldgemsparkoff', 'v_259_rubygemsparkoff', 'v_260_diamondgemsparkoff', 'v_261_ambergemsparkoff'];
const GEMSPARK_ON = ['v_262_amethystgemspark', 'v_263_topazgemspark', 'v_264_sapphiregemspark',
  'v_265_emeraldgemspark', 'v_266_rubygemspark', 'v_267_diamondgemspark', 'v_268_ambergemspark'];

/** 通电石/宝石块等的成对 tile id 切换 */
function swapTile(w: Wiring, x: number, y: number, fromKey: string, toKey: string): void {
  const st = w.st;
  if (!st.inBounds(x, y)) return;
  const i = st.idx(x, y);
  const cur = st.type[i];
  const from = TILE_BY_KEY[fromKey], to = TILE_BY_KEY[toKey];
  if (cur === from) st.setTile(x, y, to, st.frameX[i], st.frameY[i]);
  else if (cur === to) st.setTile(x, y, from, st.frameX[i], st.frameY[i]);
}

/** 多格器件整体翻帧(篝火/壁炉/吊灯等通用模式,Wiring.cs:1003-1052 等):
 *  锚点 = (x - frameX%blockW/18, y - frameY%blockH/18);delta 由锚点帧阈值定;整体 SkipWire */
function flipBlock(
  w: Wiring, x: number, y: number,
  blockW: number, blockH: number, tilesW: number, tilesH: number,
  axis: 'x' | 'y', threshold: number, delta: number,
): void {
  const st = w.st;
  const i = st.idx(x, y);
  const ox = x - Math.floor(st.frameX[i] % blockW / 18);
  const oy = y - Math.floor(st.frameY[i] % blockH / 18);
  let d = delta;
  const ai = st.idx(ox, oy);
  if (axis === 'y' ? st.frameY[ai] >= threshold : st.frameX[ai] >= threshold) d = -delta;
  for (let ix = ox; ix < ox + tilesW; ix++) {
    for (let iy = oy; iy < oy + tilesH; iy++) {
      if (!st.inBounds(ix, iy)) continue;
      const ii = st.idx(ix, iy);
      w.skipCell(ix, iy);
      if (axis === 'y') st.setTile(ix, iy, st.type[ii], st.frameX[ii], st.frameY[ii] + d);
      else st.setTile(ix, iy, st.type[ii], st.frameX[ii] + d, st.frameY[ii]);
    }
  }
}

function flipCell(w: Wiring, x: number, y: number, delta: number): void {
```

```
      mechSpawnOk: (px: number, py: number, type: number) => {
        // NPC.MechSpawn（NPC.cs:7399-7483）：同 type（等价组互计）三阈值拒绝
        const grp = NPC_MECH_GROUPS.find((g) => g.includes(type));
        let total = 0, near = 0, mid = 0;
        for (const o of this.entities.enemies) {
          const en = o as Enemy;
          const t = en.vanillaId;
          if (t == null) continue;
          if (t !== type && !(grp && grp.includes(t)) && !(type === 55 && t === 230)) continue;
          total++;
          const d = Math.hypot(en.x - px, en.y - py);
          if (d < 200) near++;
          if (d < 600) mid++;
          if (near >= 3 || mid >= 6 || total >= 10) return false;
        }
        return true;
      },
      itemSpawnOk: (px: number, py: number, type: number) => {
        // Item.MechSpawn（Item.cs:48982-49013）：同 item 三阈值拒绝
        let total = 0, near = 0, mid = 0;
        for (const o of this.entities.drops) {
          const d = o as ItemDrop;
          if (dropVanillaId(d) !== type) continue;
          total++;
          const dist = Math.hypot(d.x - px, d.y - py);
          if (dist < 300) near++;
          if (dist < 800) mid++;
          if (near >= 3 || mid >= 6 || total >= 10) return false;
        }
        return true;
      },
      shootProjectile: (px: number, py: number, vx: number, vy: number, damage: number, kind: 'dart' | 'superdart' | 'flame' | 'spear' | 'spiky' | 'boulder' | 'geyser') => {
        this.entities.add(new TrapShot(px, py, vx, vy, damage, kind), 'projectiles');
        this.sfxAt('tink', px, py); // 陷阱发射:按距离衰减
      },
      countProjectiles: (kind: 'spiky', px: number, py: number, radius: number) => {
        let n = 0;
        for (const e of this.entities.projectiles) {
          if (e instanceof TrapShot && e.kind === kind && Math.hypot(e.x - px, e.y - py) < radius) n++;
        }
        return n;
      },
      doorOpen: (x: number, y: number) => {
        // 原版 HitWireSingle 10/11:随机方向 + 反向重试(WorldGen.OpenDoor)
        const dir = Math.random() < 0.5 ? 1 : -1;
        if (!openDoor(this.world.store, x, y, dir)) return openDoor(this.world.store, x, y, -dir) ;
        return true;
      },
      doorClose: (x: number, y: number) => closeDoor(this.world.store, x, y),
      teleportPair: (a: [number, number], b: [number, number]) => this.teleportPairImpl(a, b),
      relocateTownNpc: (group: 0 | 1, px: number, py: number) => {
        // 雕像 40/41 城镇 NPC 白名单(原版两组;此处按 NPC 图鉴顺序取前几个存在的)
        const groups: Array<Array<string>> = [
          ['guide', 'merchant', 'nurse', 'demolitionist', 'dyer', 'arms_dealer'],
          ['dryad', 'painter', 'golfer', 'zoologist', 'pirate', 'stylist'],
        ];
        for (const key of groups[group]) {
          const npc = this.entities.npcs.find((n) => (n as TownNPC).npcKey === key);
          if (npc) {
            npc.x = px - npc.w / 2;
            npc.y = py - npc.h - 1;
            return true;
          }
        }
        return false;
      },
      explode: (tx: number, ty: number, damage: number, radius?: number, destroyTiles?: boolean) =>
        this.explodeAt(tx, ty, damage, radius, destroyTiles),
      toast: (m: string) => this.cb.onToast(m),
      sfx: (n: string, x?: number, y?: number) => {
        if (x !== undefined && y !== undefined) this.sfxAt(n as SfxName, x, y);
        else this.sfx.play(n as SfxName);
      },
    };
  }

  /** 传送器对(Wiring.cs:2314-2365):板上 48×48 判定框内实体双向搬迁 */
  private teleportPairImpl(a: [number, number], b: [number, number]) {
    // 太近的竖叠板拒绝(:2316)
    if (Math.abs(a[0] - b[0]) < 3 && a[1] > b[1] - 3 && a[1] < b[1]) return;
    const rect = (pad: [number, number]) => ({
      x: pad[0] * TILE, y: pad[1] * TILE - 48, w: 48, h: 48,
    });
    const hit = (r: { x: number; y: number; w: number; h: number }, e: { x: number; y: number; w: number; h: number }) =>
      e.x < r.x + r.w && e.x + e.w > r.x && e.y < r.y + r.h && e.y + e.h > r.y;
    const ra = rect(a), rb = rect(b);
    const dx = rb.x - ra.x, dy = rb.y - ra.y;
    if (!this.wiring?.blockPlayerTeleportationForOneIteration) {
      const p = this.player;
      if (!p.dead && hit(ra, p)) { p.x += dx; p.y += dy; p.vx = 0; p.vy = 0; this.sfx.play('tink'); }
      else if (!p.dead && hit(rb, p)) { p.x -= dx; p.y -= dy; p.vx = 0; p.vy = 0; this.sfx.play('tink'); }
    }
    for (const e of this.entities.enemies) {
      if (hit(ra, e)) { e.x += dx; e.y += dy; }
      else if (hit(rb, e)) { e.x -= dx; e.y -= dy; }
    }
  }

  /** 爆炸(炸药/地雷/巨石近似):半径 3 清软块 + 范围伤害 + 粒子 */
  /** 爆炸不可炸 tile 表（Projectile.CanExploteTile cs:75764 1:1）：
   *  地牢砖族 tileDungeon{41,43,44,677-679}(Main.cs:7941) + 基础宝箱 BasicChest{21,467}
   *  (TileID.cs:315) + switch 黑名单{26 祭坛,88 柜子,121/122 史莱姆块?,150 蛛网? ,
   *  211 血月砖?,226 蜥蜴砖,237 蜥蜴祭坛,248-250 尖刺?,346/470/475/504 陷阱族,685/686} */
  private static readonly EXPLODE_NEVER = new Set([41, 43, 44, 677, 678, 679, 21, 467,
    26, 88, 121, 122, 150, 211, 226, 237, 248, 249, 250, 346, 470, 475, 504, 685, 686]);
  /** 困难模式矿石（仅 scarab 弹 1086/1087 可炸——未引入恒 false） */
  private static readonly EXPLODE_HM_ORE = new Set([107, 108, 111, 221, 223]);
  /** 爆炸半径表（Projectile.cs:75262）：炸弹族=4、炸药棍族=7、放置炸药 108=10；
   *  表外（手雷 30 等）= 不破坏地形（Kill 判定表 :75260 之外） */
  static readonly EXPLODE_RADIUS: Record<number, number> = {
    28: 4, 37: 4, 516: 4, 519: 4, 29: 7, 470: 7, 637: 7, 108: 10,
  };
  /** CanExploteTile 纯函数版（测试用）：规则见 explodeAt 注释 */
  static canExplodeTile(
    st: World['store'], x: number, y: number, hardMode: boolean, golemDown: boolean, lavaLine: number,
  ): boolean {
    const t = st.get(x, y);
    const sheet = TILE_DEFS[t]?.vanilla?.sheet ?? -1;
    if (Game.EXPLODE_NEVER.has(sheet)) return false;
    const ii = st.idx(x, y);
    if (st.wall[ii] === 350) return false;
    if (Game.EXPLODE_HM_ORE.has(sheet)) return false;
    if ((sheet === 37 || sheet === 58) && !hardMode) return false;
    if (sheet === 77 && !hardMode && y >= lavaLine) return false;
    if (sheet === 137 && !golemDown) {
      const row = Math.floor(st.frameY[ii] / 18);
      if (row >= 1 && row <= 4) return false; // 神庙门行（:75805 (uint)(num-1)<=3）
    }
    return true;
  }

  /** 爆炸 1:1（Projectile.ExplodeTiles cs:75835 + CanExploteTile cs:75764）：
   *  radius=Projectile.cs:75262 半径表（炸弹族28/37/516/519=4、炸药棍族29/470/637=7、
   *  放置炸药108=10、手雷族不破坏地形→destroyTiles=false）；
   *  免疫表 EXPLODE_NEVER + 困难模式矿石 + 陨石/狱石(37/58)肉前免疫 + 黑檀石(77)肉前
   *  地狱免疫 + 蜥蜴砖(137)石巨人前神庙门行(frameY/18∈1-4)免疫 + 墙350；
   *  破坏走 breakTile（=WorldGen.KillTile 全语义，**含掉落**——曾误注释"不掉物品"）；

```

```
// 飞镖投射物(原版 Projectile 10 Dart 语义:直线飞行、撞块消失、命中伤害)。
// 陷阱(137)发射;简化布线:同排/同列直线可见(≤60 格无实心阻挡)的拉杆/开关/压力板驱动。
import { Entity } from './Entity';
import { playEnemyHitSound } from './projTargets';
import { TILE } from '../core/constants';
import type { GameHooks } from './types';
import type { Renderer } from '../render/Renderer';
import type { Camera } from '../render/Camera';

/** 电路陷阱投射物(Wiring.cs:1495-1743)。贴图 = 原版 projectile id;
 *  帧尺寸按实际解包 PNG 整图(素材为完整单帧:98 竖镖 10×28 / 184 竖火舌 10×18 /
 *  185 尖刺球 16×16 / 99 巨石 32×32)——勿按 SetDefaults 碰撞盒裁剪,否则只画残片 */
// tile 137 kind ↔ projectile 对应(Wiring.cs:1495-1743 + Item.cs placeStyle 核实):
//   0 飞镖机关(539)→98 | 1 超级飞镖机关(1146)→184(毒镖样式,有图) |
//   2 烈焰机关(1147)→187(FlamethrowerTrap,官方空桩贴图,视觉=火焰尘埃) |
//   3 尖球机关(1148)→185 | 4 长矛机关(1149)→186(有图)
// 187/654 的 PNG 是官方占位空桩(xnb 像素全零,经字节级同 stub 证实),isBlankTex 检出后
// 走色块回退 + 粒子拖尾——与原版"隐形弹体+尘埃视觉"一致(Projectile.cs:24222 188 同族 dust 驱动)
const TRAP_SHOT_STYLE: Record<string, { w: number; h: number; color: string; grav: number; proj?: string; fw?: number; fh?: number; spin?: boolean; rotOff?: number; trail?: { colors: string[]; count: number; life: number; grav: number } }> = {
  dart: { w: 8, h: 4, color: '#C8B89A', grav: 0, proj: 'vanilla/Projectile_98.png', fw: 10, fh: 28, rotOff: Math.PI / 2 },
  superdart: { w: 6, h: 6, color: '#8FBF6A', grav: 0, proj: 'vanilla/Projectile_184.png', fw: 10, fh: 18, rotOff: Math.PI / 2 },
  flame: { w: 10, h: 10, color: '#FF8030', grav: 0, proj: 'vanilla/Projectile_187.png', fw: 16, fh: 16,
    trail: { colors: ['#FF8030', '#FFC040', '#FF5010'], count: 2, life: 16, grav: -0.03 } },
  spear: { w: 6, h: 14, color: '#B8B8C0', grav: 0, proj: 'vanilla/Projectile_186.png', fw: 10, fh: 16, rotOff: Math.PI / 2 },
  spiky: { w: 12, h: 12, color: '#8A8F96', grav: 0.18, proj: 'vanilla/Projectile_185.png', fw: 16, fh: 16, spin: true },
  boulder: { w: 14, h: 14, color: '#9A8C72', grav: 0.22, proj: 'vanilla/Projectile_99.png', fw: 32, fh: 32, spin: true },
  geyser: { w: 10, h: 14, color: '#B8E8F0', grav: 0.02, proj: 'vanilla/Projectile_654.png', fw: 16, fh: 16,
    trail: { colors: ['#E8F4F8', '#C8E4EE'], count: 1, life: 26, grav: -0.015 } },
};

export class TrapShot extends Entity {
  w = 8; h = 8;
  vx: number;
  vy: number;
  damage: number;
  kind: 'dart' | 'superdart' | 'flame' | 'spear' | 'spiky' | 'boulder' | 'geyser';
  /** 滚转角(尖刺球/巨石) */
  private rot = 0;
  life = 600;
  dead = false;

  /** 全透明贴图缓存(xnb 解包异常的 187/654 等):检出一次后走色块回退,避免隐形弹幕 */
  private static blankTex = new Set<string>();
  private static okTex = new Set<string>();
  private static isBlank(r: Renderer, path: string): boolean {
    if (TrapShot.blankTex.has(path)) return true;
    if (TrapShot.okTex.has(path)) return false;
    const img = r.atlas?.vimages.get(path);
    let blank = true;
    if (img) {
      const c = document.createElement('canvas');
      c.width = img.width; c.height = img.height;
      const cx = c.getContext('2d')!;
      cx.drawImage(img, 0, 0);
      const d = cx.getImageData(0, 0, c.width, c.height).data;
      for (let i = 3; i < d.length; i += 4) { if (d[i] > 30) { blank = false; break; } }
    }
    (blank ? TrapShot.blankTex : TrapShot.okTex).add(path);
    return blank;
  }

  constructor(x: number, y: number, vx: number, vy: number, damage: number, kind: 'dart' | 'superdart' | 'flame' | 'spear' | 'spiky' | 'boulder' | 'geyser') {
    super();
    const s = TRAP_SHOT_STYLE[kind];
    this.x = x; this.y = y;
    this.vx = vx; this.vy = vy;
    this.damage = damage;
    this.kind = kind;
    this.w = s.w; this.h = s.h;
    if (kind === 'spiky') this.life = 1800; // 原版尖刺球 timeLeft 1800
  }

  draw(r: Renderer, _cam: Camera): void {
    // 世界坐标绘制(Renderer 实体循环运行在世界变换内,勿再自算屏幕坐标)
    const ctx = r.canvas.getContext('2d');
    if (!ctx) return;
    const st = TRAP_SHOT_STYLE[this.kind];
    const img = st.proj && r.atlas ? r.atlas.vimages.get(st.proj) : null;
    if (img && st.fw && st.fh && !TrapShot.isBlank(r, st.proj!)) {
      // 原版弹幕贴图(整帧):飞行类贴图默认朝上(镖尖/火苗向上),对齐速度方向需 +90°;
      // 尖刺球/巨石不随速度旋转,按位移滚转
      ctx.save();
      ctx.translate(this.x + this.w / 2, this.y + this.h / 2);
      if (st.spin) ctx.rotate(this.rot);
      else if (Math.abs(this.vx) + Math.abs(this.vy) > 0.01) ctx.rotate(Math.atan2(this.vy, this.vx) + (st.rotOff ?? 0));
      ctx.drawImage(img, 0, 0, st.fw, st.fh, -st.fw / 2, -st.fh / 2, st.fw, st.fh);
      ctx.restore();
      return;
    }
    ctx.fillStyle = st.color;
    ctx.fillRect(this.x, this.y, this.w, this.h);
  }

  fixedUpdate(_dt: number, game: GameHooks) {
    // 联机傀儡（远端弹幕快照驱动）：纯位移外推，不跑命中/碰撞（伤害归拥有者客户端结算）
    if (this.netPuppet) { this.x += this.vx; this.y += this.vy; return; }
    if (--this.life <= 0) { this.dead = true; return; }
    const st = TRAP_SHOT_STYLE[this.kind];
    if (st.spin) this.rot += this.vx * 0.03;
    this.vy += st.grav;
    this.x += this.vx;
    this.y += this.vy;
    // 粒子拖尾(原版火焰陷阱/热喷泉的视觉主体是沿途粒子;每 2 tick 一次防刷屏)
    if (st.trail && (this.life & 1) === 0) {
      game.spawnParticles(
        this.x + this.w / 2, this.y + this.h / 2,
        st.trail.colors[(this.life >> 1) % st.trail.colors.length],
        st.trail.count, 0.4, { life: st.trail.life, damp: 0.96, grav: st.trail.grav },
      );
    }
    const tx = Math.floor((this.x + this.w / 2) / TILE);
    const ty = Math.floor((this.y + this.h / 2) / TILE);
    if (game.world.store.isSolid(tx, ty)) {
      // 尖刺球/巨石:撞块反弹衰减,其余消失
      if (this.kind === 'spiky' || this.kind === 'boulder') {
        if (Math.abs(this.vy) > 1) { this.vy *= -0.4; this.vx *= 0.7; this.y -= this.vy; }
        else { this.vy = 0; this.vx *= 0.94; }
      } else { this.dead = true; return; }
    }
    const p = game.player;
    if (!p.dead && this.x < p.x + p.w && this.x + this.w > p.x && this.y < p.y + p.h && this.y + this.h > p.y) {
      p.damage(this.damage, this.x, this.y);
      game.addDamageNumber(p.cx, p.y, this.damage, false, '#FF8060');
      if (this.kind !== 'boulder') this.dead = true;
    }

    // ---- 命中怪物/小动物/城镇 NPC（原版陷阱弹幕对全实体敌对，Projectile.DamageNPC 语义） ----
    // 穿透规则（近似原版）：巨石碾压穿透；尖刺球/火焰穿透（靠受击者无敌帧节流连击）；
    // 镖/超级飞镖/热喷泉一碰即碎（与对玩家一致）
    const pierce = this.kind === 'boulder' || this.kind === 'spiky' || this.kind === 'flame';
    const overlaps = (e: { x: number; y: number; w: number; h: number }) =>
      this.x < e.x + e.w && this.x + this.w > e.x && this.y < e.y + e.h && this.y + this.h > e.y;
    // 怪物桶（Enemy.hurt 自带 8tick 无敌帧，穿透弹不会同帧连击）
    for (const ent of game.enemies()) {
      const e = ent as unknown as { x: number; y: number; w: number; h: number; dead: boolean; hurt: (d: number, kx: number, ky: number, g: GameHooks) => boolean; def?: { hitSound?: string[] } };
      if (e.dead || !overlaps(e)) continue;
      if (e.hurt(this.damage, Math.sign(this.vx) * 2, 0, game)) {
        playEnemyHitSound(game, e);
        game.addDamageNumber(this.x, this.y, this.damage, false, '#FFD060');
      }
      if (!pierce) { this.dead = true; return; }
    }
    // 小动物桶（Enemy 单参 shim / Critter.hurt(game) 皆可，被击即死）
    const buckets = (game as unknown as { entities?: { critters: Array<{ x: number; y: number; w: number; h: number; dead: boolean; hurt: (g: GameHooks) => boolean }>; npcs: Array<{ x: number; y: number; w: number; h: number; dead: boolean; hurt: (d: number, kx: number, ky: number, g: GameHooks) => boolean }> } }).entities;
    if (buckets) {
      for (const c of buckets.critters) {
        if (c.dead || !overlaps(c)) continue;
        c.hurt(game);
        if (!pierce) { this.dead = true; return; }
      }
      // 城镇 NPC（TownNPC.hurt：防御减伤+无敌帧+死亡移除）
      for (const n of buckets.npcs) {
        if (n.dead || !overlaps(n)) continue;
        n.hurt(this.damage, Math.sign(this.vx) * 2, 0, game);
        if (!pierce) { this.dead = true; return; }
      }
    }
  }
}

export class Dart extends Entity {
  w = 8; h = 4;
  vx: number;
  vy: number;
  damage: number;
  life = 600; // tick 上限(原版 timeLeft 600)
  dead = false;

  constructor(x: number, y: number, vx: number, vy: number, damage: number) {
    super();
    this.x = x; this.y = y;
    this.vx = vx; this.vy = vy;
    this.damage = damage;
  }

  draw(r: Renderer, _cam: Camera): void {
    // 世界坐标绘制(同 TrapShot)
    const ctx = r.canvas.getContext('2d');
    if (!ctx) return;
    ctx.fillStyle = '#C8B89A';
    ctx.fillRect(this.x, this.y, this.w, this.h);
  }

  fixedUpdate(_dt: number, game: GameHooks) {
    // 联机傀儡（远端弹幕快照驱动）：纯位移外推，不跑命中/碰撞（伤害归拥有者客户端结算）
    if (this.netPuppet) { this.x += this.vx; this.y += this.vy; return; }
    if (--this.life <= 0) { this.dead = true; return; }
    const world = game.world;
    this.x += this.vx;
    this.y += this.vy;
    // 撞实心块消失
    const tx = Math.floor((this.x + this.w / 2) / TILE);
    const ty = Math.floor((this.y + this.h / 2) / TILE);
    if (world.store.isSolid(tx, ty)) { this.dead = true; return; }
    // 命中玩家(敌对陷阱):矩形相交
    const p = game.player;
    if (!p.dead && this.x < p.x + p.w && this.x + this.w > p.x && this.y < p.y + p.h && this.y + this.h > p.y) {
      p.damage(this.damage, this.x, this.y);
      game.addDamageNumber(p.cx, p.y, this.damage, false, '#FF8060');
      this.dead = true;
    }
  }
}

```

```
// Unity 素材图集运行时加载器
// 数据由 scripts/build-atlas.mjs 生成（public/sprites/atlas.json + resources.json）
// 原版素材由 scripts/vanilla-atlas.mjs 生成（public/sprites/vanilla.json，独立命名空间、无 Unity y 翻转）
// 注意：Unity 精灵 rect 的 y 轴原点在【左下】，Canvas 在【左上】，取用时要翻转。
import atlasJson from '../../public/sprites/atlas.json';
import resourcesJson from '../../public/sprites/resources.json';
import vanillaJson from '../../public/sprites/vanilla.json';
import vanillaNpcsJson from '../../public/sprites/vanilla-npcs.json';
import vanillaUiJson from '../../public/sprites/vanilla-ui.json';

export interface VanillaTileMeta {
  name: string; key: string; sheet: string;
  solid: boolean; blend: boolean; framed: boolean; light: boolean;
  color: string; placement: string | null;
  grid: [number, number];      // 帧像素尺寸（蜡烛类 [16,20]）
  stride: [number, number];    // 表内帧步长（grid+gap，如 [18,18]）
  frameSize: Array<[number, number]>; // 每个 style 的占格数
  cols: number; rows: number;
  isStone?: boolean; isGrass?: boolean; mergeWith?: number | null;
}
export interface VanillaItemMeta {
  name: string; key: string; icon: string; createTile: number | null;
  /** 图集子矩形(vanilla-atlas.mjs shelf-pack 后携带;旧单体条目无此组) */
  ix?: number; iy?: number; iw?: number; ih?: number;
}
export interface VanillaWallMeta {
  name: string; key: string; sheet: string; color: string;
  grid: [number, number]; stride: [number, number]; cols: number; rows: number;
  largeFrame?: number;
}
// NPC 贴图表（纵向帧条：小动物等）
export interface VanillaNpcMeta { sheet: string; frameW: number; frameH: number; count: number; }
export interface VanillaData {
  tiles: Record<string, VanillaTileMeta>;
  items: Record<string, VanillaItemMeta>;
  walls: Record<string, VanillaWallMeta>;
  npcs?: Record<string, VanillaNpcMeta>;
  tileNames?: Record<string, string>;  // 全量原版 tile id → 英文名（兼容报告用）
  itemNames?: Record<string, string>;
  /** 盔甲贴图槽位序号（Armor_Head/Armor_Armor/Armor_Legs 的索引，非物品 id） */
  armorIndex?: Record<string, { head: number; body: number; legs: number }>;
}

/** 整图硬 alpha：alpha ≥128 → 255，<128 → 0（并清零 RGB），消除提取 PNG 的半透明镶边 */
function hardAlpha(img: HTMLImageElement): HTMLCanvasElement {
  const c = document.createElement('canvas');
  c.width = img.width; c.height = img.height;
  const ctx = c.getContext('2d')!;
  ctx.drawImage(img, 0, 0);
  const d = ctx.getImageData(0, 0, c.width, c.height);
  const px = d.data;
  for (let i = 0; i < px.length; i += 4) {
    if (px[i + 3] >= 128) px[i + 3] = 255;
    else {
      px[i] = 0; px[i + 1] = 0; px[i + 2] = 0; px[i + 3] = 0;
    }
  }
  ctx.putImageData(d, 0, 0);
  return c;
}

export class SpriteAtlas {
  data = atlasJson as unknown as AtlasData;
  resources = resourcesJson as unknown as ResourcesData;
  vanilla = vanillaJson as unknown as VanillaData;
  images = new Map<string, HTMLImageElement | HTMLCanvasElement>();
```

```
// 原版素材接入：按白名单从 terraria-assets 拷贝 PNG + 提取 TEdit 元数据 → public/sprites/vanilla.json
// 原版 Tiles 表无 .meta（不同于 Maples Unity 项目），按 textureGrid+frameGap 网格寻址：
//   帧 (col,row) 位于 (col*(gridX+gapX), row*(gridY+gapY))，帧尺寸 = textureGrid
// 用法：node scripts/vanilla-atlas.mjs
import { readFileSync, writeFileSync, copyFileSync, mkdirSync, existsSync, readdirSync, unlinkSync } from 'node:fs';
import { join, dirname } from 'node:path';
import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';

const here = dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url));
const ROOT = join(here, '..');
const ASSETS = join(ROOT, '..', 'terraria-assets', 'Images');
const TEDIT_DATA = join(ROOT, '..', 'Terraria-Map-Editor', 'src', 'TEdit.Terraria', 'Data');
const OUT_DIR = join(ROOT, 'public', 'sprites', 'vanilla');
const OUT_JSON = join(ROOT, 'public', 'sprites', 'vanilla.json');
const whitelist = JSON.parse(readFileSync(join(here, 'vanilla-whitelist.json'), 'utf8'));

/** 读 PNG IHDR（8 字节签名 + 4 长度 + 4 类型后即宽高，各 4 字节大端） */
function pngSize(file) {
  const b = readFileSync(file);
  return { w: b.readUInt32BE(16), h: b.readUInt32BE(20) };
}

const tiles = JSON.parse(readFileSync(join(TEDIT_DATA, 'tiles.json'), 'utf8'));
const items = JSON.parse(readFileSync(join(TEDIT_DATA, 'items.json'), 'utf8'));
const walls = JSON.parse(readFileSync(join(TEDIT_DATA, 'walls.json'), 'utf8'));
const tileById = new Map(tiles.map((t) => [t.id, t]));
const itemById = new Map(items.map((i) => [i.id, i]));
const wallById = new Map(walls.map((w) => [w.id, w]));
```

```
// 实体与 Game 交互的接口（避免循环依赖，只放类型）
import type { World } from '../world/World';

export interface GameHooks {
  world: World;
  /** 主角(投射物命中判定用) */
  player: import('./Player').Player;
  /** 敌人列表(投射物命中判定用) */
  enemies(): unknown[];
  /** 小动物列表(弹幕命中判定用;近战挥砍在 Game 内直接遍历桶) */
  critters(): unknown[];
  /** 在世界上生成掉落物（返回实体便于染色等后处理） */
  spawnDrop(x: number, y: number, itemKey: string, count?: number, vx?: number, vy?: number): import('./ItemDrop').ItemDrop | null;
  /** 对玩家造成伤害（attacker 用于荆棘反伤） */
  damagePlayer(dmg: number, srcX: number, srcY: number, attacker?: unknown): void;
  /** 伤害数字飘字 */
  addDamageNumber(x: number, y: number, amount: number, crit: boolean, color?: string): void;
  /** 弹幕砍 tileCut 物（Projectile.CutTiles 语义：箭扫过杂草/瓦罐 → Game 内部分流） */
  cutTile(x: number, y: number): void;
  /** 敌人死亡 */
  onEnemyKilled(key: string, enemy?: import('../entities/Enemy').Enemy): void;
  /** 出生敌人 */
  spawnEnemy(key: string, x: number, y: number): void;
  /** 粒子 */
  spawnParticles(
    x: number, y: number, color: string, count: number, speed?: number,
    opts?: { life?: number; damp?: number; grav?: number },
  ): void;
  /** 拾取物品后刷新 UI（掉落物实体调用） */
  notifyInventoryChanged(): void;
  /** 播放音效 */
  // 世界坐标声（原版 SoundEngine.PlaySound 带 x/y）：x/y 省略 = UI/玩家动作声不衰减
  playSfx(name: string, vol?: number, x?: number, y?: number): void;
  playSfxFiles(files: string[], vol?: number, x?: number, y?: number): void; // 怪物专属声效（原版文件名列表）
  /** 拾取物品时在主角旁飘出物品名 */
  showPickupLabel(itemKey: string): void;
  /** 掉落系统补充上下文（灵魂/钥匙/风筝等条件）：Game 实现，缺省则这些条件按 false */
  dropContext?(): import('../drops/NpcDrops').DropExtras;
  /** 月事件上下文（南瓜月/霜月 wave-gate 掉落条件）：Game 实现，缺省按无事件（恒 false） */
  moonEventCtx?(): { kind: 1 | 2; wave: number } | null;
}
```

```
// 音效：原版 wav 素材（public/sounds/）+ WebAudio 合成兜底。
// **按需加载（与贴图懒加载同策略，2026-08-10）**：不预读任何文件——播放未命中时
// 后台 fetch+decode（pending 防重入、failed 负缓存防 404 循环），首播走合成兜底/静默，
// 解码完成后后续播放命中真声。定向预热用 preloadFiles（如 Boss 召唤前取咆哮声）。
// hit 敌人受击 / hurt 玩家受击 / pickup 拾取 / dig 挖掘 / place 放置 / drink 饮用 / chop 砍树 / splash 落水
// + killed 怪物死亡 / zombie 僵尸环境声 / roar Boss咆哮 / coin 拾币 / door_open door_close / tink 敲石 / shatter 玻璃破碎 / drown 溺水
export type SfxName =
  | 'hit' | 'hurt' | 'pickup' | 'dig' | 'place' | 'drink' | 'chop' | 'splash'
  | 'killed' | 'pkilled' | 'zombie' | 'roar' | 'coin' | 'door_open' | 'door_close' | 'tink' | 'shatter' | 'drown'
  | 'menuOpen' | 'menuClose' | 'menuTick'
  | 'bowShoot'   // 原版 SoundID.Item5（弓弦，Item.cs case 99 UseSound）
  | 'throw'      // 原版 SoundID.Item1（手里剑/投掷刀 UseSound）
  | 'explosion'  // 原版 SoundID.Item14（爆炸通用：手雷/炸弹/炸药/巨石,Projectile.cs 20+ 处）
  | 'beeSummon'  // 原版 SoundID.Item173（Abeemination 蜂后召唤音，Player.cs:43185——Boss 召唤音唯一非 Roar 例外）
  | 'thunder';   // 原版 SoundID 43（Thunder_0-5，NewLightning 的 thunderDelay 归零时播）

// 逻辑名 → 原版 wav 文件（多文件随机选 + 音高扰动）
const WAV_MAP: Record<SfxName, string[]> = {
  hit: ['NPC_Hit_1', 'NPC_Hit_2', 'NPC_Hit_3', 'NPC_Hit_4', 'NPC_Hit_5'],
  hurt: ['Player_Hit_0', 'Player_Hit_1', 'Player_Hit_2'],
  killed: ['NPC_Killed_1', 'NPC_Killed_2', 'NPC_Killed_3'],
  pkilled: ['Player_Killed'],
  zombie: ['Zombie_1', 'Zombie_2', 'Zombie_3', 'Zombie_4', 'Zombie_5', 'Zombie_6'],
  roar: ['Roar_0', 'Roar_1'],
  pickup: ['Grab'],
  dig: ['Dig_0', 'Dig_1', 'Dig_2'],
  chop: ['Grass'],
  tink: ['Tink_0', 'Tink_1'],
  place: ['Dig_1', 'Dig_2'],
  shatter: ['Shatter'],
  coin: ['Coin_0', 'Coin_1', 'Coins'],
  door_open: ['Door_Opened'],
  door_close: ['Door_Closed'],
  splash: ['Splash_0', 'Splash_1'],
  drown: ['Drown'],
  bowShoot: ['Item_5'],
  // 菜单 UI（原版 id 10=Menu_Open / id 11=Menu_Close / id 12=Menu_Tick）
  menuOpen: ['Menu_Open'],
  menuClose: ['Menu_Close'],
  menuTick: ['Menu_Tick'],
  throw: ['Item_1'],
  explosion: ['Item_14'],
  beeSummon: ['Item_173'],
  thunder: ['Thunder_0', 'Thunder_1', 'Thunder_2', 'Thunder_3', 'Thunder_4', 'Thunder_5'],
  // 以下无原版素材，走合成
  drink: [],
};

export class Sfx {
  private ctx: AudioContext | null = null;
  private buffers = new Map<string, AudioBuffer>();
  /** 在途解码中的文件（防重复 fetch） */
  private pending = new Set<string>();
  /** 取回失败/不存在的文件（负缓存，防 404 每次重试） */
  private failed = new Set<string>();
  muted = false;
  /** 主音量 0..1（M6 设置页） */
  master = 1;

  /** 按需取缓冲：未命中时后台 fetch+decode，同步返回已就位缓冲或 null */
  private ensureBuffer(file: string): AudioBuffer | null {
    const buf = this.buffers.get(file);
    if (buf) return buf;
    if (this.pending.has(file) || this.failed.has(file)) return null;
    const ctx = this.ensure(true);
    if (!ctx) return null;
    this.pending.add(file);
    fetch(`sounds/${file}.wav`)
      .then((r) => (r.ok ? r.arrayBuffer() : null))
      .then((ab) => (ab ? ctx.decodeAudioData(ab) : null))
      .then((decoded) => {
        if (decoded) this.buffers.set(file, decoded);
        else this.failed.add(file);
      })
      .catch(() => { this.failed.add(file); })
      .finally(() => { this.pending.delete(file); });
    return null;
  }

  /** 定向预热（不阻塞）：把指定 wav 后台取齐，播放时即命中 */

```

````
AI specifics to implement per kind (commented with vanilla lines):
- gravDelay: cannonball/bunny gravity only after 18 ticks (Projectile.cs:21546-21550 / 26844-26849); snowball after 20 ticks with 0.3 (Projectile.cs:21879-21896); X damping 0.99 (cannonball/bunny) / 0.98 (snowball).
- So TRAP_SHOT_STYLE gains fields: `gravDelay?: number; xDamp?: number; blast?: number (half-extent px);`
- rotation: spin uses rot += vx*0.03 currently; vanilla for aiStyle2: rotation += (|vx|+|vy|)*0.03*direction (Projectile.cs:21528); bunny: rotation += vx*0.05 + direction*0.05 (Projectile.cs:26841). I'll add a `spinMode` or compute: for these kinds use (|vx|+|vy|)*0.03*dir for cannonball/snowball; bunny vx*0.05+dir*0.05. Add optional `spinFn`? - Tile hit: cannonball/bunny → explode AoE (vanilla Kill: Projectile.cs:72768-72831 for 162 = 64x64 Damage() then 144x144 Damage(); 72704-72758 for 281 = 128x128 Damage() when damage>0), play 'explosion' sound (Item14), particles, dead. Snowball → shatter (sound Item51≈? we have 'shatter'; vanilla SoundID.Item51 — check what Item51 is: probably a soft "plop"? We can use 'shatter'... hmm Item51 might be "squeaky toy"? Let me not overthink: use 'shatter' with comment) and die without AoE.

- Direct-hit behavior: cannonball penetrate = 4 (vanilla) — hits up to 4 NPCs then dies? For vanilla, projectile with penetrate 4 despawns after 4 hits... Actually projectile.Kill on hitting NPC happens when penetrate runs out. Cannonball hitting an NPC: vanilla projectile keeps going and explodes after penetrate exhausted. Simplify: cannonball/bunny explode on first NPC hit? In vanilla, the cannonball does NOT explode on NPC hit — it continues (penetrate 4) and explodes when it dies (tile hit / timeLeft). Our approximation: on NPC hit, deal damage and continue (pierce), explode on tile hit or life end. Life: timeLeft default 3600 for cannonball — our TrapShot.life=600 default; set 3600 for cannonball, 600 for bunny (SetDefaults timeLeft 600), 3600 for snowball? Snowball default 3600 too. Vanilla json 281 timeLeft 600.

Explosion AoE implementation inside TrapShot: damage all enemies/critters/npcs/player overlapping a box of half-extent `blast` px centered at impact; spawn particles; play explosion sfx via game.playSfx('explosion', 1, x, y)? Check playSfx signature: playSfx(name, vol?, x?, y?). Let me verify Game's playSfx implements distance attenuation. And TrapShot elsewhere uses playEnemyHitSound(game, e). Fine.

Blast half-extents: cannonball: vanilla expands to 144x144 → half 72. But it damages twice: 64x64 then 144x144 (two Damage passes — same damage applied twice to entities in the smaller box? Damage() applies damage to each overlapping entity; two calls = entities in the 64x64 box take damage twice, subject to immunity frames). Simplify: single pass with 144x144 box (half 72) — comment the vanilla double-pass. Bunny: 128x128 → half 64.

Friendly note: cannonballs are friendly projectiles (don't hurt the player) — vanilla friendly=true means they hurt NPCs, not the player (unless getGoodWorld). Our TrapShot currently damages the player for all kinds (it's used by hostile traps). For the new cannon kinds, we must NOT damage the player. So add a `friendly?: boolean` flag in style: cannonball/bunny/snowball/confetti friendly=true → skip player damage, damage enemies (and town NPCs? friendly projectiles don't hit town NPCs — vanilla Projectile.Damage skips town NPCs for friendly... For the new kinds I'll skip town NPCs and critters? Friendly projectiles do kill critters? Hmm — vanilla: friendly projectiles don't hit town NPCs or critters generally (town NPCs are immune to friendly fire; critters can be hit?). To keep it simple and safe: friendly kinds damage only enemies bucket. Comment accordingly.
````

```
// 结构与行号对照:
//   updateMech        Wiring.cs:88-161(计时器/引爆器冷却,每 tick 调)
//   hitSwitch         Wiring.cs:163-252(手动/物理触发源)
//   checkMech         Wiring.cs:313-327(陷阱冷却注册,上限 999)
//   xferWater         Wiring.cs:329-377(泵配对液体转移)
//   tripWire          Wiring.cs:379-529(四色 pass 顺序 红→蓝→绿→黄)
//   pixelBoxPass      Wiring.cs:531-553
//   logicGatePass     Wiring.cs:555-591
//   checkLogicGate    Wiring.cs:593-691(六种门 + 故障灯)
//   hitWire           Wiring.cs:693-827(BFS;邻居序 下/上/右/左;分线盒预算)
//   hitWireSingle     Wiring.cs:829-2282(核心 case 在此,其余委托 devices 表)

//   massWireOperation :2407-2558(宏伟蓝图 L 形铺线)
// 单机移植:NetMessage/SendData 一律跳过(原版联机同步,无操作)。
import { TileStore } from './TileStore';
import { TILE_DEFS } from '../data/tiles';

/** 器件分发表(P3 devices.ts 提供):返回 true 表示已处理该 case */
export type DeviceHandler = (w: Wiring, sheet: number, x: number, y: number) => boolean;

/** 多色工具模式位(WiresUI.Settings.MultiToolMode) */
export const TOOL_RED = 1;
export const TOOL_GREEN = 2;
export const TOOL_BLUE = 4;
export const TOOL_YELLOW = 8;
export const TOOL_ACTUATOR = 16;
export const TOOL_CUTTER = 32;

const MAX_PUMP = 20;   // Wiring.cs:34(MaxPump=20,填充上限 19)
const MAX_MECH = 1000; // Wiring.cs:41(MaxMech=1000,注册上限 999)

export class Wiring {
  readonly st: TileStore;
  /** 器件表(P3 注入) */
  device: DeviceHandler | null = null;
  /** 传送器对执行(引擎侧搬迁玩家/敌人;P3 注入) */
  teleportPair: ((a: [number, number], b: [number, number]) => void) | null = null;
  /** 世界地表行(蜥蜴砖致动限制 Wiring.cs:2375) */
  worldSurface = 0;
  /** 世世纪杀花已击败(同上) */
  planteraDowned = false;

  running = false;                                  // :23
  blockPlayerTeleportationForOneIteration = false;  // :22(测重板抑制传送)
  private currentWireColor = 0;                     // :46(1红 2蓝 3绿 4黄;彩线灯泡用)

  // BFS 状态(:24-27)。点用 idx(y*w+x) 编码。
  private wireSkip = new Set<number>();
  private wireList: number[] = [];      // FIFO(head 指针)
  private wireDir: number[] = [];       // 与 wireList 平行的进入方向 0下 1上 2右 3左
  private toProcess = new Map<number, number>(); // :27 分线盒再访预算

  // 逻辑门(:28-32)
  private gatesCurrent: number[] = [];
  private gatesNext: number[] = [];
  private gatesDone = new Map<number, boolean>();
  private lampsToCheck: number[] = [];
  private pixelBoxTriggers = new Map<number, number>();

  // 传送器对(:33;px 坐标,-1 = 未捕获)与泵(:34-40)
  private tp: Array<[number, number]> = [[-1, -1], [-1, -1]];
  private inPumpX = new Int16Array(MAX_PUMP); private inPumpY = new Int16Array(MAX_PUMP);
  private outPumpX = new Int16Array(MAX_PUMP); private outPumpY = new Int16Array(MAX_PUMP);
  private numInPump = 0; private numOutPump = 0;

  // 冷却注册(:41-45)
  private mechX = new Int32Array(MAX_MECH + 1);
  private mechY = new Int32Array(MAX_MECH + 1);
  private mechTime = new Int32Array(MAX_MECH + 1);
  private numMechs = 0;

  constructor(st: TileStore) {
    this.st = st;
  }

  private p2i(x: number, y: number): number { return y * this.st.w + x; }
  private i2x(i: number): number { return i % this.st.w; }
  private i2y(i: number): number { return (i / this.st.w) | 0; }
  private sheetAt(x: number, y: number): number { return this.sheetOf(x, y); }
```

```
  checkMech(i: number, j: number, time: number): boolean {
    for (let k = 0; k < this.numMechs; k++) {
      if (this.mechX[k] === i && this.mechY[k] === j) return false;
    }
    if (this.numMechs >= 999) return false;
    this.mechX[this.numMechs] = i;
    this.mechY[this.numMechs] = j;
    this.mechTime[this.numMechs] = time;
    this.numMechs++;
    return true;
  }

  // ============ HitSwitch(Wiring.cs:163-252)============

  hitSwitch(i: number, j: number): void {
    if (!this.st.inBounds(i, j)) return;
    const sh = this.sheetAt(i, j);
```

```
  private sheetAt(x: number, y: number): number { return this.sheetOf(x, y); }
  /** 该格的原版 tile id(sheet;非 vanilla tile 返回 -1)。器件表按 sheet 分发 */
  sheetOf(x: number, y: number): number {
    if (!this.st.inBounds(x, y)) return -1;
    const d = TILE_DEFS[this.st.type[this.p2i(x, y)]];
    return d?.vanilla?.sheet ?? -1;
```

```
  readonly h: number;
  readonly type: Uint16Array;
  readonly flags: Uint8Array; // bit0 active bit1 framedDecor占位(预留)
  readonly frameX: Uint16Array;
  readonly frameY: Uint16Array;
  readonly wall: Uint16Array; // 原版 wld 墙 id（0-366）
  readonly liquid: Uint8Array;   // 0-255 液量
  readonly liquidType: Uint8Array; // 0无 1水 2岩浆 3蜂蜜 4微光(原版 liquidType 3,本仓库 +1 编码;任意液体遇微光 → Aetherium 块 659)
  readonly half: Uint8Array;      // halfBrick 位（原版 Tile.cs bTileHeader bit1）：下半 8px 实心
  readonly slope: Uint8Array;     // 坡面 0-4（原版编码；渲染暂未支持，生成期少用）
  readonly wire: Uint8Array;      // 电路位（原版 Tile.cs sTileHeader）：bit0红 bit1蓝 bit2绿 bit3黄 bit4致动器 bit5已致动(inActive)
  // wire 位掩码常量（对应原版 Tile.cs:396-459 的 wire/wire2/wire3/wire4/actuator/inActive）
  static readonly WIRE_RED = 1;
  static readonly WIRE_BLUE = 2;
  static readonly WIRE_GREEN = 4;
  static readonly WIRE_YELLOW = 8;
  static readonly WIRE_ACTUATOR = 16;
  static readonly WIRE_ACTUATED = 32;
  static readonly WIRE_COLORS = 15; // 四色掩码

  // tile 变更监听（chunk 缓存 / 光照脏标记 / 液体模拟统一从这里拿事件）
  private listeners: Array<(x: number, y: number) => void> = [];

  // ---- 多人联机上报钩子（ClientNet 注入；单机为 null 零开销） ----
  /** 本地 tile 变更上报（协议层 TileOp 收集器）。liquid 通道不报（客户端本地模拟） */
  netReporter: ((op: import('../net/protocol').TileOp) => void) | null = null;
  /** 应用远端操作期间置位：上报钩子静默（防回环） */
  netSuppress = false;

  constructor(w: number, h: number, bufs?: import('../workers/protocol').TileBuffers) {
    this.w = w; this.h = h;
    // buffers 注入（worker transfer 重建路径）：直接以移交的 ArrayBuffer 包装 TypedArray，
    // 跳过分配与拷贝。缺省 explored 由调用方（World.fromPacket）补齐
    if (bufs) {
      this.type = new Uint16Array(bufs.type);
      this.flags = new Uint8Array(bufs.flags);
      this.frameX = new Uint16Array(bufs.frameX);
      this.frameY = new Uint16Array(bufs.frameY);
      this.wall = new Uint16Array(bufs.wall);
      this.liquid = new Uint8Array(bufs.liquid);
      this.liquidType = new Uint8Array(bufs.liquidType);
      this.half = new Uint8Array(bufs.half);
      this.slope = new Uint8Array(bufs.slope);
      this.wire = new Uint8Array(bufs.wire);
      return;
    }
    const n = w * h;
    this.type = new Uint16Array(n);
    this.flags = new Uint8Array(n);
    this.frameX = new Uint16Array(n);
    this.frameY = new Uint16Array(n);
    this.wall = new Uint16Array(n);
    this.liquid = new Uint8Array(n);
    this.liquidType = new Uint8Array(n);
```

```
export function deviceTable(w: Wiring, ctx: DeviceCtx, sheet: number, x: number, y: number): boolean {
  const st = w.st;
  const i = st.idx(x, y);
  switch (sheet) {
    case 4: // 火把 frameX ±66(:1207-1213)
      flipCell(w, x, y, 66);
      return true;
    case 10: // 关门 → 开(随机方向+反向,原版 :1171-1188)
      ctx.doorOpen(x, y);
      return true;
    case 11:
      ctx.doorClose(x, y);
      return true;
    case 15: { // 马桶(frameY/40 判定,:1194-1204;喷水近似粒子)
      if (Math.floor(st.frameY[i] / 40) !== 1 && Math.floor(st.frameY[i] / 40) !== 20) return true;
      if (w.checkMech(x, y, 60)) ctx.sfx('splash', x * 16 + 8, y * 16 + 8);
      return true;
    }
    case 33: case 174: case 49: case 372: // 蜡烛族 frameX ±18(:1472-1480)
  }
  return false;
}

/** 装配:把器件表挂到 Wiring(器件 case 之外的默认落点) */
export function attachDevices(w: Wiring, ctx: DeviceCtx): void {
  w.device = (ww, sheet, x, y) => deviceTable(ww, ctx, sheet, x, y);
  w.teleportPair = (a, b) => ctx.teleportPair(a, b);
}

```

````
if (st.spin) this.rot += this.vx * 0.03;  // existing for spiky/boulder
```
New: for cannon kinds, rotation per vanilla:
- cannonball/snowball (aiStyle 2, Projectile.cs:21528): rotation += (|vx|+|vy|) * 0.03 * direction
- bunny (aiStyle 49, Projectile.cs:26841): rotation += vx*0.05 + direction*0.05

I'll add style field `spin?: boolean | 'roll' | 'bunny'`. Implementation:

```ts
// 滚转：尖刺球/巨石沿用旧近似；炮弹族按原版
if (st.spin === true) this.rot += this.vx * 0.03;
else if (st.spin === 'roll') this.rot += (Math.abs(this.vx) + Math.abs(this.vy)) * 0.03 * (this.vx < 0 ? -1 : 1); // Projectile.cs:21528
else if (st.spin === 'bunny') this.rot += this.vx * 0.05 + (this.vx < 0 ? -1 : 1) * 0.05; // Projectile.cs:26841
```

Gravity:
```ts
// 炮弹族：前 gravDelay tick 直线（无重力），之后 vy += grav / vx *= xDamp
if (st.gravDelay !== undefined) {
  this.age++;
  if (this.age >= st.gravDelay) { this.vy += st.grav; this.vx *= st.xDamp ?? 1; }
} else {
  this.vy += st.grav;
}
```
Need an `age` counter starting at 0. Note gravDelay semantics: vanilla ai[0] increments each AI tick and applies gravity when ai[0] >= 18 → the 19th tick onward. Our age++ then check age >= 18 → same.

/** 落点爆炸（Projectile.Kill :72768-72831 type162 / :72704-72758 type281）：
 *  纯伤害（不破坏地形——162/281 均不在 ExplodeTiles 判定表 Projectile.cs:75260），
 *  原版两段伤害盒 64×64→144×144（162）/128×128（281），此处合并为一段 blast 半宽盒 */
private blastKill(game: GameHooks) {
  this.dead = true;
  const cx = this.x + this.w / 2, cy = this.y + this.h / 2;
  const r = TRAP_SHOT_STYLE[this.kind].blast!;
  game.playSfx('explosion', 1, cx, cy);   // SoundID.Item14（Projectile.cs:72769）
  game.spawnParticles(cx, cy, '#FFA050', 12, 3, { life: 18, damp: 0.93, grav: -0.02 });
  game.spawnParticles(cx, cy, '#FFF0C0', 8, 4, { life: 10, damp: 0.9, grav: -0.05 });
  const box = { x: cx - r, y: cy - r, w: 2 * r, h: 2 * r };
  const hit = (e: {x,y,w,h}) => overlap(box, e);
  for (const ent of game.enemies()) { ...e.hurt(this.damage, ...) }
  // 城镇 NPC/小动物：友方弹幕不误伤（原版 friendly 弹对城镇 NPC 免疫 NPC.cs）
}
```
````

```
  private explodeAt(tx: number, ty: number, damage: number, radius = 3, destroyTiles = true) {
    const st = this.world.store;
    const R = radius;
    const hardMode = !!this.world.flags.hardMode;
    const golemDown = !!this.world.flags['downed_245'];
    const canExplode = (x: number, y: number): boolean =>
      Game.canExplodeTile(st, x, y, hardMode, golemDown, this.world.lavaLine);
    // 半径内被炸的格：多格 framed 物体按整件清除（锚点邻域扫描，同 breakTile 语义），
    // 防半件残留可重复破坏；炸药(141/142)收集后链式引爆（原版连爆语义，上限 32 防递归）
    const chain: Array<[number, number]> = [];
    if (destroyTiles && R > 0) {
      // ShouldWallExplode(:75739)：盘内任一格无墙 → 墙也可炸
      let wallSplode = false;
      for (let dx = -R; dx <= R && !wallSplode; dx++) {
        for (let dy = -R; dy <= R; dy++) {
          if (dx * dx + dy * dy > R * R) continue;
          const x = tx + dx, y = ty + dy;
          if (st.inBounds(x, y) && st.wall[st.idx(x, y)] === 0) { wallSplode = true; break; }
        }
      }
      for (let dx = -R; dx <= R; dx++) {
        for (let dy = -R; dy <= R; dy++) {
          if (dx * dx + dy * dy > R * R) continue;
          const x = tx + dx, y = ty + dy;
          if (!st.inBounds(x, y)) continue;
          const t = st.get(x, y);
          if (t !== 0) {
            if (!canExplode(x, y)) continue;
            if (t === TILE_BY_KEY['v_141_explosives']) { chain.push([x, y]); continue; }
            this.breakTile(x, y); // KillTile 全语义（掉落/树/罐/幼虫/蜂巢…）
          }
          if (wallSplode) {
            // 每 destroyed 格 3×3 清墙（:75852-75867，墙≠350）
            for (let wx = x - 1; wx <= x + 1; wx++) {
              for (let wy = y - 1; wy <= y + 1; wy++) {
                if (!st.inBounds(wx, wy)) continue;
                const wi = st.idx(wx, wy);
                if (st.wall[wi] > 0 && st.wall[wi] !== 350) st.setWallSilent(wx, wy, 0);
              }
            }
          }
        }
      }
    }
    const cx = tx * TILE + 8, cy = ty * TILE + 8;
    for (const e of this.entities.enemies) {
      if (Math.hypot(e.x - cx, e.y - cy) < R * TILE + 16) { const en = e as Enemy; if (en.hurt) en.hurt(damage, cx - (e.cx ?? cx), cy - (e.cy ?? cy), this); }
    }
    const p = this.player;
    if (Math.hypot(p.cx - cx, p.cy - cy) < R * TILE + 16) p.damage(damage, cx, cy);
    this.spawnParticles(cx, cy, '#FFA040', 26, 4);
    // 原版爆炸声 SoundID.Item14(Projectile.cs 手雷/炸弹/炸药等 20+ 处)——
    // 曾误用 roar(Boss 咆哮)
    this.sfxAt('explosion', cx, cy);
    // 链式引爆（延后到本爆结算后，防爆心递归；上限防炸药墙死循环）
    let chained = 0;
    while (chain.length && chained < 32) {
      const [ex, ey] = chain.pop()!;
      if (st.get(ex, ey) !== TILE_BY_KEY['v_141_explosives']) continue;
      st.setTile(ex, ey, 0);
      chained++;
      this.explodeAt(ex, ey, 500, 10);

```

````
3. angle 1: base (7.5,-2.5), len = sqrt(56.25+6.25)=sqrt(62.5)=7.9057 → vx = 7.5/7.9057*14 = 13.2813..., vy = -4.4271...

7. snowball 212: place 3x3, hit col 1 row 1 (num51==1 → num56=0). Facing band: frameX/54. For anchor cells frameX = facing*54 + col*18. Hit col 1 → frameX = facing*54+18. num55 = frameX/54 = facing (since 18 < 54). Facing 1 (right): dx positive (85..104 → after normalize positive), px = (ox+2)*16 - 8 + 12, py = (oy+2)*16-8. damage 35, kind 'snowball', speed in [12,16.5). checkMech(ox,oy,60) → immediate re-hit no shot; cooldown 15 set.

8. TrapShot physics unit test: create TrapShot cannonball with vx=14,vy=0 in an empty world (need GameHooks mock). Gravity delayed 18 ticks, then vy += 0.28 & vx *= 0.99. Snowball: 20 ticks, 0.3/0.98. Blast on tile hit: enemy within 72px takes damage; player NOT damaged (friendly).
````

```
import { World } from '../src/world/World';
import { Game } from '../src/core/Game';
import { TILE_BY_KEY, TILE_DEFS } from '../src/data/tiles';

// 爆炸族群 1:1（Projectile.CanExploteTile cs:75764 / ExplodeTiles cs:75835 / 半径表 cs:75262）
describe('爆炸规则（CanExploteTile + 半径表）', () => {
  const w = () => {
    const world = new World(40, 40, 42, 'open');
    world.lavaLine = 30;
    return world;
  };
  const place = (world: World, key: string, x: number, y: number, frameY = 0) => {
    const t = TILE_BY_KEY[key]!;
    world.store.setTileSilent(x, y, t, 0, frameY);
    return t;
  };
  const sheetOf = (t: number) => TILE_DEFS[t]?.vanilla?.sheet ?? -1;
  const can = (world: World, key: string, x: number, y: number, o: { hard?: boolean; golem?: boolean; fy?: number } = {}) =>
    Game.canExplodeTile(world.store, x, y, !!o.hard, !!o.golem, world.lavaLine);

  it('普通块可炸（石/泥土/黑曜石）', () => {
    const world = w();
    place(world, 'stone', 20, 20);
    place(world, 'obsidian', 21, 20);
    expect(can(world, 'stone', 20, 20)).toBe(true);
    expect(can(world, 'obsidian', 21, 20)).toBe(true);
    expect(sheetOf(TILE_BY_KEY['stone']!)).toBe(1);
  });

  it('永不可炸：地牢砖族(41/44) + 宝箱(21) + 祭坛(26) + 蜥蜴祭坛(237) + 蜥蜴砖(226)', () => {
    const world = w();
    place(world, 'v_41_blue_brick', 20, 20);
    place(world, 'v_44_pink_brick', 21, 20);
    place(world, 'v_26_altars', 22, 20);
    place(world, 'v_237_lihzahrd_altar', 23, 20);
    place(world, 'v_226_lihzahrd_brick', 24, 20);
    expect(can(world, 'v_41_blue_brick', 20, 20, { hard: true })).toBe(false);
    expect(can(world, 'v_44_pink_brick', 21, 20, { hard: true })).toBe(false);
    expect(can(world, 'v_26_altars', 22, 20, { hard: true })).toBe(false);
    expect(can(world, 'v_237_lihzahrd_altar', 23, 20, { hard: true, golem: true })).toBe(false);
    expect(can(world, 'v_226_lihzahrd_brick', 24, 20, { hard: true, golem: true })).toBe(false);
  });

  it('陨石(37)：肉前免疫、肉后可炸（cs:75795）', () => {
    const world = w();
    place(world, 'ore_meteorite', 20, 20);
    expect(sheetOf(TILE_BY_KEY['ore_meteorite']!)).toBe(37);
    expect(can(world, 'ore_meteorite', 20, 20)).toBe(false);
    expect(can(world, 'ore_meteorite', 20, 20, { hard: true })).toBe(true);
  });
```

```
140	    if (this.netPuppet) { this.x += this.vx; this.y += this.vy; return; }
141	    if (--this.life <= 0) { this.dead = true; return; }
142	    const st = TRAP_SHOT_STYLE[this.kind];

144	    this.vy += st.grav;
145	    this.x += this.vx;
146	    this.y += this.vy;

148	    if (st.trail && (this.life & 1) === 0) {
149	      game.spawnParticles(
150	        this.x + this.w / 2, this.y + this.h / 2,
151	        st.trail.colors[(this.life >> 1) % st.trail.colors.length],
152	        st.trail.count, 0.4, { life: st.trail.life, damp: 0.96, grav: st.trail.grav },
153	      );
154	    }
155	    const tx = Math.floor((this.x + this.w / 2) / TILE);
156	    const ty = Math.floor((this.y + this.h / 2) / TILE);
157	    if (game.world.store.isSolid(tx, ty)) {
158	      // 尖刺球/巨石:撞块反弹衰减,其余消失
159	      if (this.kind === 'spiky' || this.kind === 'boulder') {
160	        if (Math.abs(this.vy) > 1) { this.vy *= -0.4; this.vx *= 0.7; this.y -= this.vy; }
161	        else { this.vy = 0; this.vx *= 0.94; }
162	      } else { this.dead = true; return; }
163	    }
164	    const p = game.player;
165	    if (!p.dead && this.x < p.x + p.w && this.x + this.w > p.x && this.y < p.y + p.h && this.y + this.h > p.y) {
166	      p.damage(this.damage, this.x, this.y);
167	      game.addDamageNumber(p.cx, p.y, this.damage, false, '#FF8060');
168	      if (this.kind !== 'boulder') this.dead = true;
169	    }
170

172	    // 穿透规则（近似原版）：巨石碾压穿透；尖刺球/火焰穿透（靠受击者无敌帧节流连击）；
173	    // 镖/超级飞镖/热喷泉一碰即碎（与对玩家一致）
174	    const pierce = this.kind === 'boulder' || this.kind === 'spiky' || this.kind === 'flame';
175	    const overlaps = (e: { x: number; y: number; w: number; h: number }) =>
176	      this.x < e.x + e.w && this.x + this.w > e.x && this.y < e.y + e.h && this.y + this.h > e.y;
177	    // 怪物桶（Enemy.hurt 自带 8tick 无敌帧，穿透弹不会同帧连击）

180	      if (e.dead || !overlaps(e)) continue;
181	      if (e.hurt(this.damage, Math.sign(this.vx) * 2, 0, game)) {

189	    if (buckets) {

199	        if (!pierce) { this.dead = true; return; }
200	      }
201	    }
202	  }
```

```json
 "new_string": "  fixedUpdate(_dt: number, game: GameHooks) {\n    // 联机傀儡（远端弹幕快照驱动）：纯位移外推，不跑命中/碰撞（伤害归拥有者客户端结算）\n    if (this.netPuppet) { this.x += this.vx; this.y += this.vy; return; }\n    if (--this.life <= 0) {\n      // 炮弹族 timeLeft 耗尽同样走 Kill 爆炸（Projectile.Kill 由 timeLeft 归零触发）\n      if (TRAP_SHOT_STYLE[this.kind].blast) this.blastKill(game);\n      else this.dead = true;\n      return;\n    }\n    const st = TRAP_SHOT_STYLE[this.kind];\n    this.age++;\n    // 首帧发射音（Projectile.AI：162=Projectile.cs:21542-21546 / 281=26830-26833 SoundID.Item14）\n    if (this.age === 1 && st.launchSfx) game.playSfx(st.launchSfx, 1, this.x + this.w / 2, this.y + this.h / 2);\n    // 滚转：尖刺球/巨石近似 | 炮弹族按原版\n    if (st.spin === true) this.rot += this.vx * 0.03;\n    else if (st.spin === 'roll') {\n      // aiStyle2（Projectile.cs:21528）：rotation += (|vx|+|vy|)*0.03*direction\n      this.rot += (Math.abs(this.vx) + Math.abs(this.vy)) * 0.03 * (this.vx < 0 ? -1 : 1);\n    } else if (st.spin === 'bunny') {\n      // aiStyle49（Projectile.cs:26841）：rotation += vx*0.05 + direction*0.05\n      this.rot += this.vx * 0.05 + (this.vx < 0 ? -1 : 1) * 0.05;\n    }\n    if (st.gravDelay !== undefined) {\n      // 炮弹族弧线：前 gravDelay tick 直线，之后 vy+=grav / vx*=xDamp\n      // （162/281=Projectile.cs:21546-21550/26844-26849 的 ai[0]>=18；166=:21879-21884 的 >=20）\n      if (this.age >= st.gravDelay) {\n        this.vy += st.grav;\n        this.vx *= st.xDamp ?? 1;\n        if (st.vCap !== undefined && this.vy > st.vCap) this.vy = st.vCap; // 281 :26850-26853\n      }\n    } else {\n      this.vy += st.grav;\n    }\n    this.x += this.vx;\n    this.y += this.vy;\n    // 粒子拖尾(原版火焰陷阱/热喷泉的视觉主体是沿途粒子;每 2 tick 一次防刷屏)\n    if (st.trail && (this.life & 1) === 0) {\n      game.spawnParticles(\n        this.x + this.w / 2, this.y + this.h / 2,\n        st.trail.colors[(this.life >> 1) % st.trail.colors.length],\n        st.trail.count, 0.4, { life: st.trail.life, damp: 0.96, grav: st.trail.grav },\n      );\n    }\n    const tx = Math.floor((this.x + this.w / 2) / TILE);\n    const ty = Math.floor((this.y + this.h / 2) / TILE);\n    if (game.world.store.isSolid(tx, ty)) {\n      // 尖刺球/巨石:撞块反弹衰减\n      if (this.kind === 'spiky' || this.kind === 'boulder') {\n        if (Math.abs(this.vy) > 1) { this.vy *= -0.4; this.vx *= 0.7; this.y -= this.vy; }\n        else { this.vy = 0; this.vx *= 0.94; }\n      } else if (st.blast) {\n        // 炮弹/兔兔炮撞块 → Kill 落点爆炸（Projectile.cs:72768-72831 / 72704-72758）\n        this.blastKill(game);\n        return;\n      } else {\n        if (st.shatterDust) {\n          // 雪球碎裂（Projectile.cs:71758-71767）：尘 76 十粒 + Item51\n          game.spawnParticles(this.x + this.w / 2, this.y + this.h / 2, '#F2F8FF', 10, 1, { life: 20, damp: 0.94, grav: -0.01 });\n          game.playSfx('shatter', 1, this.x, this.y);\n        }\n        this.dead = true;\n        return;\n      }\n    }\n    // 友方弹幕（炮弹族 friendly=true）：不伤玩家，直接进敌怪判定\n    if (!st.friendly) {\n      const p = game.player;\n      if (!p.dead && this.x < p.x + p.w && this.x + this.w > p.x && this.y < p.y + p.h && this.y + this.h > p.y) {\n        p.damage(this.damage, this.x, this.y);\n        game.addDamageNumber(p.cx, p.y, this.damage, false, '#FF8060');\n        if (this.kind !== 'boulder') this.dead = true;\n        if (this.dead) return;\n      }\n    }"
}
```

## 🤖 Assistant · 2026-08-11T10:01:12.968Z · glm-x-preview-260804

<details open>
<summary>💭 thinking</summary>

```
Now the hit section below needs updating: friendly kinds should skip critters/town NPCs and pierce (cannonball penetrate 4, bunny -1, snowball 1). Currently the code continues into the enemy/critter/npc loops with `pierce` computed. Let me restructure: compute per-kind pierce including new kinds, and skip critter/npc buckets for friendly kinds.

Vanilla semantics:
- cannonball penetrate 4: hits up to 4 NPCs then dies (Projectile: on NPC hit, numHits++, when >= penetrate → Kill → explosion!). Actually in vanilla, when a projectile's penetrate is exhausted it calls Kill() → the cannonball explodes. So a cannonball that hits 4 enemies explodes at the 4th hit. Our approximation: cannonball pierces and explodes on tile hit / life end. To keep it manageable, I'll implement: cannonball pierces enemies (no death on hit) — the explosion still happens at impact. Comment the deviation.
- bunny penetrate -1 (infinite): never dies on NPC hit; explodes on tile/life.
- snowball: penetrate default 1 → dies on first NPC hit (vanilla: Kill on hit with penetrate 1 → shatter, no AoE). So snowball = non-pierce, dies on enemy hit with shatter.

So: pierce = boulder/spiky/flame/cannonball/bunny. snowball non-pierce. And friendly skip of critters/npcs.

For the snowball shatter on enemy hit — reuse a small helper `shatterKill(game)`? Keep it simple: on non-pierce hit, `this.dead = true` (existing). Shatter dust only on tile hit is fine (vanilla Kill does dust regardless of cause, but let me add dust for snowball death on enemy hit too via a check before setting dead). Simpler: in the non-pierce branches, if st.shatterDust → spawn dust. There are three places (enemies/critters/npcs). Since friendly kinds skip critters/npcs, snowball death happens in the enemy loop only. I'll add shatter there.

Let me now update the enemy-hit section.
```

```
229	    // 小动物桶（Enemy 单参 shim / Critter.hurt(game) 皆可，被击即死）
230	    const buckets = (game as unknown as { entities?: { critters: Array<{ x: number; y: number; w: number; h: number; dead: boolean; hurt: (g: GameHooks) => boolean }>; npcs: Array<{ x: number; y: number; w: number; h: number; dead: boolean; hurt: (d: number, kx: number, ky: number, g: GameHooks) => boolean }> } }).entities;
231	    if (buckets) {
232	      for (const c of buckets.critters) {
233	        if (c.dead || !overlaps(c)) continue;
234	        c.hurt(game);
235	        if (!pierce) { this.dead = true; return; }
236	      }
237	      // 城镇 NPC（TownNPC.hurt：防御减伤+无敌帧+死亡移除）
238	      for (const n of buckets.npcs) {
239	        if (n.dead || !overlaps(n)) continue;
240	        n.hurt(this.damage, Math.sign(this.vx) * 2, 0, game);
241	        if (!pierce) { this.dead = true; return; }
242	      }
243	    }
244	  }
245	}
246	
247	export class Dart extends Entity {
248	  w = 8; h = 4;
249	  vx: number;
250	  vy: number;
251	  damage: number;
252	  life = 600; // tick 上限(原版 timeLeft 600)

257	    this.x = x; this.y = y;
258	    this.vx = vx; this.vy = vy;
259	    this.damage = damage;
260	  }
261

265	    if (!ctx) return;
266	    ctx.fillStyle = '#C8B89A';
267	    ctx.fillRect(this.x, this.y, this.w, this.h);
```

```json
 "old_string": "    // ---- 命中怪物/小动物/城镇 NPC（原版陷阱弹幕对全实体敌对，Projectile.DamageNPC 语义） ----\n    // 穿透规则（近似原版）：巨石碾压穿透；尖刺球/火焰穿透（靠受击者无敌帧节流连击）；\n    // 镖/超级飞镖/热喷泉一碰即碎（与对玩家一致）\n    const pierce = this.kind === 'boulder' || this.kind === 'spiky' || this.kind === 'flame';\n    const overlaps = (e: { x: number; y: number; w: number; h: number }) =>\n      this.x < e.x + e.w && this.x + this.w > e.x && this.y < e.y + e.h && this.y + this.h > e.y;\n    // 怪物桶（Enemy.hurt 自带 8tick 无敌帧，穿透弹不会同帧连击）\n    for (const ent of game.enemies()) {\n      const e = ent as unknown as { x: number; y: number; w: number; h: number; dead: boolean; hurt: (d: number, kx: number, ky: number, g: GameHooks) => boolean; def?: { hitSound?: string[] } };\n      if (e.dead || !overlaps(e)) continue;\n      if (e.hurt(this.damage, Math.sign(this.vx) * 2, 0, game)) {\n        playEnemyHitSound(game, e);\n        game.addDamageNumber(this.x, this.y, this.damage, false, '#FFD060');\n      }\n      if (!pierce) { this.dead = true; return; }\n    }\n    // 小动物桶（Enemy 单参 shim / Critter.hurt(game) 皆可，被击即死）\n    const buckets = (game as unknown as { entities?: { critters: Array<{ x: number; y: number; w: number; h: number; dead: boolean; hurt: (g: GameHooks) => boolean }>; npcs: Array<{ x: number; y: number; w: number; h: number; dead: boolean; hurt: (d: number, kx: number, ky: number, g: GameHooks) => boolean }> } }).entities;\n    if (buckets) {\n      for (const c of buckets.critters) {\n        if (c.dead || !overlaps(c)) continue;\n        c.hurt(game);\n        if (!pierce) { this.dead = true; return; }\n      }\n      // 城镇 NPC（TownNPC.hurt：防御减伤+无敌帧+死亡移除）\n      for (const n of buckets.npcs) {\n        if (n.dead || !overlaps(n)) continue;\n        n.hurt(this.damage, Math.sign(this.vx) * 2, 0, game);\n        if (!pierce) { this.dead = true; return; }\n      }\n    }\n  }\n}",
 "new_string": "    // ---- 命中怪物/小动物/城镇 NPC（原版陷阱弹幕对全实体敌对，Projectile.DamageNPC 语义） ----\n    // 穿透规则（近似原版）：巨石碾压穿透；尖刺球/火焰穿透（靠受击者无敌帧节流连击）；\n    // 镖/超级飞镖/热喷泉一碰即碎（与对玩家一致）；\n    // 炮弹族：162 penetrate4 / 281 penetrate-1（SetDefaults）→ 穿透，撞块/超时才 Kill 爆炸；\n    // 166 雪球 penetrate1 → 一碰即碎\n    const pierce = this.kind === 'boulder' || this.kind === 'spiky' || this.kind === 'flame'\n      || this.kind === 'cannonball' || this.kind === 'bunny';\n    const overlaps = (e: { x: number; y: number; w: number; h: number }) =>\n      this.x < e.x + e.w && this.x + this.w > e.x && this.y < e.y + e.h && this.y + this.h > e.y;\n    // 怪物桶（Enemy.hurt 自带 8tick 无敌帧，穿透弹不会同帧连击）\n    for (const ent of game.enemies()) {\n      const e = ent as unknown as { x: number; y: number; w: number; h: number; dead: boolean; hurt: (d: number, kx: number, ky: number, g: GameHooks) => boolean; def?: { hitSound?: string[] } };\n      if (e.dead || !overlaps(e)) continue;\n      if (e.hurt(this.damage, Math.sign(this.vx) * 2, 0, game)) {\n        playEnemyHitSound(game, e);\n        game.addDamageNumber(this.x, this.y, this.damage, false, '#FFD060');\n      }\n      if (!pierce) {\n        if (st.shatterDust) this.shatter(game);\n        this.dead = true;\n        return;\n      }\n    }\n    // 友方弹幕（炮弹族 friendly=true）到此为止：不误伤小动物/城镇 NPC\n    // （原版 friendly 弹对城镇 NPC 免疫；小动物不在敌怪判定内）\n    if (st.friendly) return;\n    // 小动物桶（Enemy 单参 shim / Critter.hurt(game) 皆可，被击即死）\n    const buckets = (game as unknown as { entities?: { critters: Array<{ x: number; y: number; w: number; h: number; dead: boolean; hurt: (g: GameHooks) => boolean }>; npcs: Array<{ x: number; y: number; w: number; h: number; dead: boolean; hurt: (d: number, kx: number, ky: number, g: GameHooks) => boolean }> } }).entities;\n    if (buckets) {\n      for (const c of buckets.critters) {\n        if (c.dead || !overlaps(c)) continue;\n        c.hurt(game);\n        if (!pierce) { this.dead = true; return; }\n      }\n      // 城镇 NPC（TownNPC.hurt：防御减伤+无敌帧+死亡移除）\n      for (const n of buckets.npcs) {\n        if (n.dead || !overlaps(n)) continue;\n        n.hurt(this.damage, Math.sign(this.vx) * 2, 0, game);\n        if (!pierce) { this.dead = true; return; }\n      }\n    }\n  }\n\n  /** 雪球碎裂（Projectile.Kill type166，Projectile.cs:71758-71767：尘 76 十粒 + Item51） */\n  private shatter(game: GameHooks): void {\n    game.spawnParticles(this.x + this.w / 2, this.y + this.h / 2, '#F2F8FF', 10, 1, { life: 20, damp: 0.94, grav: -0.01 });\n    game.playSfx('shatter', 1, this.x, this.y);\n  }\n\n  /** 落点爆炸（Projectile.Kill：type162=Projectile.cs:72768-72831 / type281=72704-72758）：\n   *  纯范围伤害（162/281 均不在 ExplodeTiles 判定表 Projectile.cs:75260 → 不破坏地形）；\n   *  原版两段伤害盒 162 为 64×64→144×144、281 为 128×128，此处按 blast 半宽合并一段；\n   *  friendly 弹不伤玩家（hostile 伤害仅在 getGoodWorld 下对玩家生效，Main.getGoodWorld） */\n  private blastKill(game: GameHooks): void {\n    this.dead = true;\n    const st = TRAP_SHOT_STYLE[this.kind];\n    const cx = this.x + this.w / 2, cy = this.y + this.h / 2;\n    game.playSfx('explosion', 1, cx, cy); // SoundID.Item14（Projectile.cs:72769/72706）\n    game.spawnParticles(cx, cy, '#FFA050', 14, 3.5, { life: 18, damp: 0.93, grav: -0.02 });\n    game.spawnParticles(cx, cy, '#FFF0C0', 8, 4.5, { life: 10, damp: 0.9, grav: -0.05 });\n    const r = st.blast!;\n    const box = { x: cx - r, y: cy - r, w: 2 * r, h: 2 * r };\n    for (const ent of game.enemies()) {\n      const e = ent as unknown as { x: number; y: number; w: number; h: number; dead: boolean; hurt: (d: number, kx: number, ky: number, g: GameHooks) => boolean; def?: { hitSound?: string[] } };\n      if (e.dead) continue;\n      if (!(box.x < e.x + e.w && box.x + box.w > e.x && box.y < e.y + e.h && box.y + box.h > e.y)) continue;\n      if (e.hurt(this.damage, Math.sign(this.vx) * 2, 0, game)) {\n        playEnemyHitSound(game, e);\n        game.addDamageNumber(cx, cy, this.damage, false, '#FFD060');\n      }\n    }\n  }\n}"
}
```

```
// 弹幕命中目标:怪物桶之外还有小动物桶(critters)。
// 原版小动物是 NPC(NPCID.Squirrel 等),任何伤害即死——近战挥砍已覆盖
// (Game 挥砍段"小动物:任何挥击一击致死"),弹幕此前漏判,箭射松鼠无效果。
import type { GameHooks } from './types';

export interface CritterLike {
  x: number; y: number; w: number; h: number;
  dead: boolean;
  /** 小动物受击:一击致死,返回是否生效(iframes 内 false) */
  hurt(game: GameHooks): boolean;
}

/** 弹幕与实体 AABB 重叠 */
export function projOverlap(ax: number, ay: number, aw: number, ah: number, e: { x: number; y: number; w: number; h: number }): boolean {
  return ax < e.x + e.w && ax + aw > e.x && ay < e.y + e.h && ay + ah > e.y;
}

/** 命中小动物:一击致死。返回是否命中(调用方按各自语义消耗穿透/销毁弹体) */
export function hitCritters(self: { x: number; y: number; w: number; h: number }, game: GameHooks): boolean {
  const list = (game as unknown as { critters?: () => unknown[] }).critters?.() ?? [];
  for (const ent of list) {
    const c = ent as CritterLike;
    if (c.dead) continue;
    if (projOverlap(self.x, self.y, self.w, self.h, c)) {
      c.hurt(game);
      return true;
    }
  }
  return false;
}

/** 弹幕命中怪物:播受击音效(原版 NPC.StrikeNPC 语义——任意伤害来源都响,
 *  近战挥砍早已如此;弹幕此前漏了) */
export function playEnemyHitSound(game: GameHooks, e: { def?: { hitSound?: string[] } }): void {
  const files = e.def?.hitSound;
  if (files && files.length) game.playSfxFiles(files, 0.9);
}

```

## 🤖 Assistant · 2026-08-11T10:04:15.073Z · glm-x-preview-260804

<details open>
<summary>💭 thinking</summary>

```
My devices.ts checks the bunny gate before setting the cooldown (I placed the BunnyCannonCanFire check before the cooldown assignment). In vanilla, the order is: cooldown set (Wiring.cs:1334-1339) happens BEFORE ShootFromCannon (1341), and BunnyCannonCanFire is inside ShootFromCannon (50697). So vanilla sets cooldown even when the bunny gate refuses. My implementation refuses before setting the cooldown — a deviation.

Fix: move the bunny gate check after the cooldown assignment (i.e., right before shootProjectile but after cooldown set). Let me reorder.
```

</details>
